| R65.31 | Where does a crew that breaks under Air Bursts (D5.311) go? | Pass 65; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-23 ("start on Pass 65 without my intervention"), open to a veto. It stays inside its AFV, broken, in the Crew role, with the Armor Leader dormant; the AFV Stops, and its fire and movement are barred (`play.vehicle-crew-broken`). In the RtPh it must rout beneath, through the rout-beneath path, and the AFV is Abandoned when it does; its broken Passengers must rout beneath too. Made at the reviews (the first referee's finding 8, the table player's 11), built in fix Round A. |
| R65.32 | May a capturing squad become a Temporary crew? | Pass 65; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-23 ("start on Pass 65 without my intervention"), open to a veto. A21.22's free exchange of a capturing squad for a HS is not built in this pass. The refusal says so plainly ("a squad's exchange for a HS (A21.22) is not built: Deploy it, or let a HS or crew enter"), and a backlog row (71.2) records it. Made at the reviews (the table player's finding 9), built in fix Round D. |
| R65.33 | Does leadership modify the Scrounging dr? (revised at fix Round B) | Pass 65; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-23 ("start on Pass 65 without my intervention"), open to a veto. Revised at fix Round B: no. Scrounging takes no leadership: A.10's ∆. D10.5's "1-3 dr (∆)" carries the triangle, and A.10 (p. 43) says leadership ratings do not modify the DR or dr of the actions marked with it. The second referee's finding 10 and this ruling's first wording (the best Good Order leader's modifier as a drm) read the ∆ backwards; the fixing agent caught it on the page, and the session confirmed it there. |
| R70.1 | Is a pass four times the plan's size kept whole, and in what order is it built? | Pass 70; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-25 ("Start Pass 70 ... Run it without my intervention, up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. Yes: it stays one pass, as R65.1 kept pass 65. The frame's estimates sum to 33:05 of building against the plan's 7:20. Built in the design's increments 3a to 3j, so that whatever a session's end left out would be the last increment's (Street Fighting, A11.52's Passengers, A18.12 against a vehicle, D5.42). Nothing was left out: the build took 8:07 by the clock. |
| R70.2 | What leaves the pass? | Pass 70; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-25 ("Start Pass 70 ... Run it without my intervention, up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. CC against a wreck goes with the rest of D10.1 to pass 110 (a wreck's own CC sequence is D10's, not A11's); Withdrawal to another level of a building to pass 135; naming an AFV in a Location that also holds concealed or hidden units or a Dummy to pass 75 (task 75.6); the SMC crew's Stun wound in the projector to pass 75 (task 75.3); an Infantry OVR on a lone SMC revealed outside the reviewed building case to pass 75; to the backlog, with the passes that build them: 5/8" Dummies, Cavalry, horses, and bicycles under concealment, a vehicle's secret Target Size, the Smoke Dispenser, entrenching, a Fire, and Clearance as actions that cost a "?", Searching for minefields and Booby Traps and a Fortified Building's reveal, a rout's destination refigured when an enemy becomes Known, and the minefield, FFE, shellhole, entrenchment, and Blaze rout options. Each has a row in the backlog's section 72.1. |
| R70.3 | Is the morale check outside fire one member or several? | Pass 70; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-25 ("Start Pass 70 ... Run it without my intervention, up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. One Rules member, `ScenarioA1MoraleCheck`, on a check-only branch of `ScenarioA1FireCalculator`: the existing resolution of a check on its subjects, then the leader-loss cascade, with no IFT roll. Every MC, NMC, PTC, LLMC, and LLTC made outside the Fire and Rally packages calls it (the Interdiction NMC, the Bail Out NMC, D5.311's crew attack, the LLMC after a leader lost to Mopping Up, a Sniper, Rally Fate, a PF firer's Casualty Reduction, Failure to Rout, or an Interdiction), so Heat of Battle, ELR, wounds, and the cascade are decided once. The OVR and Deployment NTCs and PAATC keep their paths. |
| R70.4 | How is a check outside an attack recorded? | Pass 70; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-25 ("Start Pass 70 ... Run it without my intervention, up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. By a new event `morale-check-resolved`, with its facts and verdict as fire records carry theirs; its effects are written through the Fire planner's effect events. A check appended after an existing record (Heat of Battle after an Interdiction NMC's Original 2, every LLMC outside fire) is new, so it needs no gating fact: an older game holds none and replays as it did. |
| R70.5 | Where does a rout end when Heat of Battle rallies the routing unit or makes it berserk? | Pass 70; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-25 ("Start Pass 70 ... Run it without my intervention, up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. In the Location where it was Interdicted. Since 3c the same holds when a LLMC after the Voluntary Rout leader's loss eliminates, rallies, berserks, or surrenders the HS left. |
| R70.6 | Does a leader in the hex a unit routs into add his DRM to its Interdiction NMC? | Pass 70; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-25 ("Start Pass 70 ... Run it without my intervention, up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. No: only the Voluntary Rout leader routing with it does (A10.711). R70.44 keeps this way of it beside its own. |
| R70.7 | How do Heat of Battle and ELR know a target is Unarmed? | Pass 70; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-25 ("Start Pass 70 ... Run it without my intervention, up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. A new optional fact `unarmed` on each target of a fire attack and a morale check, read by Heat of Battle's subject (A15.1) and by ELR immunity (A19.11); since R70.47 also by the MC's result. Absent means as before, so an older attack resolves as it did. |
| R70.8 | How do PRC take leadership and LLMC in Fire? | Pass 70; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-25 ("Start Pass 70 ... Run it without my intervention, up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. `LeaderReaches` (D6.651) is read in the check both ways; a Passenger leader's loss checks the fellow Passengers of his vehicle only; Riders neither take nor cause LLMC; Heat of Battle for PRC stays none (A15.1). The facts are new optional ones on the PRC entries (`vehicleMoving`, `ceInUnmovedHalftrack`), so an older attack resolves as it did. In Fire a CE leader in an unmoved halftrack does not lead the Location's units on foot (the PRC attack resolves after their checks): a backlog row. |
| R70.9 | What does the Bail Out NMC take? | Pass 70; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-25 ("Start Pass 70 ... Run it without my intervention, up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. A Rider leader's DRM (A10.7's EXC), Wound Severity for a SMC, and ELR Replacement, never Disruption (A19.12), through R70.3's member. Its `drm` and `elr` are recorded on `rider-bailed-out`, and the projector compares them only when present. The pass 65 row's Heat of Battle is closed as not owed (A15.1). |
| R70.10 | What does D5.311's attack do to a crew already broken? | Pass 70; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-25 ("Start Pass 70 ... Run it without my intervention, up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. It eliminates it by Casualty Reduction; a recorded fact `crewBroken` on the vehicle target says the crew was broken, absent meaning as before. Its Heat of Battle, ELR, and leadership are not owed (A15.1, A19.11, D3.44). |
| R70.11 | How is Voluntary Break built? | Pass 70; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-25 ("Start Pass 70 ... Run it without my intervention, up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. As an action of the RtPh's start, before any rout step of the phase, by the owner of units (even pinned) in the LOS and Normal Range of an armed, unbroken Known enemy ground unit or ADJACENT to any unbroken enemy ground unit; refused when breaking would Reduce or eliminate the unit at once, and for heroes, berserk units, and Unarmed units (A10.3). It writes `voluntary-break`, then the units broken and under DM (A10.62); a leader's Voluntary Break causes a LLTC (A10.2). A concealed unit that breaks loses its "?" (A12.14), and breaking clears a pin (A7.83). |
| R70.12 | How is Voluntary Rout built? | Pass 70; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-25 ("Start Pass 70 ... Run it without my intervention, up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. As a `withLeader` argument on the rout action: a non-berserk, non-pinned, unbroken leader stacked with the broken unit before it routs moves with it, takes no NMC, adds his DRM to its Interdiction NMC (recorded in `rout-interdicted`'s `drm` with the leader named), and is eliminated if it fails, which causes a LLMC through R70.3. |
| R70.13 | May a routing unit ignore its own building? (citation corrected at 3d) | Pass 70; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-25 ("Start Pass 70 ... Run it without my intervention, up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. Yes: a routing unit may ignore the hexes of the building it starts in when choosing its destination (A10.51's EXC, pp. 66 to 67). The frame cited "A10.532's EXC, p. 67"; the 3d agent read on the page that the exception is A10.51's, which runs from p. 66 to the top of p. 67, and the code cites A10.51. Corrected in the design at the documents step. |
| R70.14 | How do vehicles and Guns Interdict? | Pass 70; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-25 ("Start Pass 70 ... Run it without my intervention, up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. Within their Normal Range (the longest of their weapons, at most 16) when no positive DRM would apply to their attack; they enter must-rout and RtPh DM as "unit/its-SW/Gun" (A10.5). Interdiction is decided at plan time and recorded, so no record fact is needed. |
| R70.15 | How is Fanaticism given by SSR? | Pass 70; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-25 ("Start Pass 70 ... Run it without my intervention, up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. A `game-started` special rule `fanatic:<side>`, optionally `:<group>`, setting `asl:fanatic` at setup; absent-safe. Units created after setup are not covered: a backlog row. |
| R70.16 | Who sees, and how is it read? | Pass 70; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-25 ("Start Pass 70 ... Run it without my intervention, up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. One Rules member, `ScenarioA1Sight`, with three purposes: Loss (Good Order, not a Dummy, not hidden, not a Passenger, not an empty vehicle, A12.14), GainDenial (unbroken, Dummies included, not hidden, not an empty vehicle, A12.122), and Inspection (Good Order ground units, A12.16). One Play read, the nearest seer's range by (side, purpose, Location), memoized per state in `PlayStepMemo`, serves every caller. A Nondefinitive, null, or unsupported LOS read is taken as each caller took it before. Reveals are recorded events, so no older record changes; only new proposals do. |
| R70.17 | Which actions cost a "?"? (corrected on the page before any build) | Pass 70; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-25 ("Start Pass 70 ... Run it without my intervention, up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. Every built "other activity" a concealed unit engages in while in the LOS and within 16 hexes of a Good Order enemy ground unit (A12.141, p. 78): Deploy, Recombine, SMOKE grenades, a LLTC or LLMC, a PF or MOL check, a leadership modifier applied to anything but a PAATC, Recover, Transfer, Dismantle, Scrounge, Hook Gun, and the Mopping Up declaration. A12.141's exceptions stay free. The frame first wrote that a check costs none; A12.141 names LLTC and LLMC among its examples, and the ruling was corrected on the page before any build. |
| R70.18 | How is the Right of Inspection by LOS shown? | Pass 70; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-25 ("Start Pass 70 ... Run it without my intervention, up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. After setup closes, an enemy stack not concealed and out of the LOS of every Good Order ground unit of the viewing side is shown as a stack is before play: its top counter, the rest a sealed count, its equipment withheld. Play gives the set of such Locations per (state, viewer), memoized, and one Play entry builds every view. A null or unsupported LOS counts as in LOS. Events and records are unchanged. |
| R70.19 | Does the Right of Inspection change older games' views? | Pass 70; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-25 ("Start Pass 70 ... Run it without my intervention, up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. Yes, every game's, with no marker on the record: the view is derived and never recorded, A12.16 is a gap the plan says to build, and a marker would keep the fault in the user's games. The gate's sweep builds the view through the new entry, and each differing view line is 70.10's. |
| R70.20 | How does the owner remove a "?" by choice? | Pass 70; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-25 ("Start Pass 70 ... Run it without my intervention, up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. By an action at any time in his own or the enemy's phase: it writes `concealment-lost` for the units; a Dummy so removed is eliminated; a hidden unit is placed and revealed in full (A12.31). The Sniper exception is noted and not built: a backlog row. |
| R70.21 | Where is the viewer's option not to force a loss built? | Pass 70; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-25 ("Start Pass 70 ... Run it without my intervention, up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. Where every Loss seer of the viewing side within 16 is itself concealed: a pending choice to that side at R70.16's read, its brief reveal shown to the moving side only. Built for a move step by day that rolls nothing; the advance, fire, night, and rolling steps keep R31d.2's deviation, as the ruling allowed, with a backlog row. |
| R70.22 | Who gains a "?" and enters concealed under 70.5? | Pass 70; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-25 ("Start Pass 70 ... Run it without my intervention, up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. An Emplaced Gun's crew and a vehicle gain "?" under the Concealment Table's Cases J and K (read from a render of the table), with A12.122's +1 Lax, -1 Stealthy, and a possessed Gun's US#. A vehicle's Concealment Terrain gains the woods-, orchard-, brush-, and grain-road hexes at setup only (A12.2). HIP is allowed for vehicles by SSR (A12.3), and an Emplaced Gun may set up hidden out of Concealment Terrain, revealed when an enemy sees it (A12.34). Its clause on reinforcements was widened by R70.35. |
| R70.23 | What do Bypass and detection do at their endings? | Pass 70; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-25 ("Start Pass 70 ... Run it without my intervention, up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. A Minimum Move into a concealed enemy's Location is forced back, all MF spent. A stack forced back from a Bypass exit keeps its lane (the step's `bypass`, absent meaning as before) and reveals every concealed unit in the Location; a Bypass member broken or pinned there reveals them; attempting to occupy an obstacle that holds concealed units reveals them and forces the stack back into its lane, MF spent; a firer in a Bypassed hex may fire TPBF at a unit in Bypass there; a vehicle ending its MPh in Bypass of a Location with concealed enemy units reveals them unless it is "broken" (A12.1). Hidden units stay hidden (A12.42 names concealed ones). |
| R70.24 | How is Searching built? | Pass 70; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-25 ("Start Pass 70 ... Run it without my intervention, up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. As an action of the moving stack as it ends its move: one more MF in its hex, a Search dr with A12.152's drm, the hexes named in the ATTACKER's order of preference before the dr, and (since R70.42) the named hexes searched first, then the others, up to the Accessible count less the Final dr. Accessible hexes are its own and the ADJACENT hexes the stack could enter. The searchers are TI for the rest of the Player Turn; concealed enemy units revealed lose "?", hidden ones are placed beneath "?", Dummies are removed; Search Casualties roll at once, one dr per Search dr, reusing Mopping Up's Casualty members. |
| R70.25 | Which buildings may be Mopped Up, and how are they found? | Pass 70; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-25 ("Start Pass 70 ... Run it without my intervention, up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. Any multi-hex or multi-level building of the map, found by the Studio's flood fill moved into Play and kept per board handle; a building a card names keeps the card's id, so older records keep theirs, and any other takes "board:lowest hex". Control folds for any building. One enumerator of the occupied Locations on `ObjectIndex` serves Searching and Mopping Up. |
| R70.26 | What bars a withdrawal, and what does it meet? | Pass 70; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-25 ("Start Pass 70 ... Run it without my intervention, up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. Only a Known enemy unit, read from the side's view. Into a concealed (not Dummy) enemy's Location the withdrawer is eliminated, a Random Selection of the enemy units there loses "?", and hidden units are placed (A11.21 and its example); into a Dummy-only Location the Dummies are removed. A unit above its IPC may withdraw by dropping SW, which stay unpossessed in the Location. |
| R70.27 | May a Gun's crew be fought in CC? | Pass 70; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-25 ("Start Pass 70 ... Run it without my intervention, up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. Yes: it is advanced against and fights with its inherent FP (A1.123); the Gun is never used in CC (A11.13), and each Gun of a unit eliminated in CC is eliminated on a colored dr of 1 followed by a dr at or below the black Kill Number, otherwise left unpossessed. The five refusals are lifted. The Guns each unit possesses are a recorded fact of the CC resolution (`guns`); absent, the Gun loss is not rolled, as before. |
| R70.28 | How does a Carrier HS recombine? | Pass 70; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-25 ("Start Pass 70 ... Run it without my intervention, up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. With no leader and no TC (A1.32 names "Carrier HS"): the leaving crew's `instance-created` records the vehicle it left (`crewOf`), absent meaning an ordinary HS. |
| R70.29 | Are `GameProjector.CheckInvariants` rules or plumbing? | Pass 70; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-25 ("Start Pass 70 ... Run it without my intervention, up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. Plumbing: UNIT-STATE-011, 012, and 014 guard the object graph and decide no outcome; A20.5's decisions are already in Rules. They stay in Units, and the backlog row of pass 32.i is closed with this ruling. Their 12% of a replay (pass 31d) stays a performance item. |
| R70.30 | How is Infantry against Infantry CC fought in a Location with a vehicle? | Pass 70; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-25 ("Start Pass 70 ... Run it without my intervention, up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. It joins the vehicle sequence (A11.31, the H3 example): a sequential round, one attack by the side named next, no simultaneous reply; each unit attacks once per CCPh, being attacked by Infantry and by a vehicle counted apart (A11.12's EXC). An Ambush there takes the vehicle drm (+2 Vehicle, +1 for each BU or Stunned AFV, A11.4), the ambusher resolving all its attacks first (A11.34), and the -1 and +1 in vehicle CC both ways (A11.51). The Ambush's vehicle drm is a recorded fact, null where no vehicle stands. |
| R70.31 | How do PRC take part in CC? | Pass 70; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-25 ("Start Pass 70 ... Run it without my intervention, up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. As `ScenarioA1PassengerActionRules.CloseCombat` decides: a Rider attacks apart with +1 and defends with -1 (A11.611); a CE halftrack's Passengers join the vehicle's attack (A11.62); the rest are offered nothing. Only the PRC of an Immobile vehicle are held in Melee at the CCPh's end (A11.71); a crew inside never is. Since fix Round A the CCPh-ending record carries a flag, `prcMelee`, and the projector reads A11.71 from the vehicle, so the public record names no unit aboard; absent means as before. |
| R70.32 | Is a CC counter placed on an unload, a Bail Out, or TPBF into an enemy Location? | Pass 70; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-25 ("Start Pass 70 ... Run it without my intervention, up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. No stored condition: units are held in Melee only at the CCPh's end already (D6.5, D6.24, A7.211). The record line says the units are in an enemy Location and not held in Melee, citing the rule. The TPBF fire record line is a backlog row. |
| R70.33 | How is Street Fighting built? | Pass 70; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-25 ("Start Pass 70 ... Run it without my intervention, up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. A vehicle in a road hex ADJACENT to building hexes on both sides of the road may be attacked in CC by Infantry advancing from the ground level of those buildings with -1, the vehicle's attack on them +1, not with an actual Ambush; afterwards they return to the Location they came from or stay, unless broken or pinned (A11.8). The building flank is a Maps read; where the unit came from is a state fact the projector sets from the advance. Its Reaction Fire (D7.211) stays with pass 105, Bypass with pass 100. |
| R70.34 | How are a vehicle's Passengers captured, a leader created against a vehicle, and a captured vehicle entered? | Pass 70; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-25 ("Start Pass 70 ... Run it without my intervention, up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. A11.52: ordinary Infantry CC at the Passengers of an unarmored stationary vehicle, -2 on attacks at them with the capture's +1, and +2 on their attacks at the captors; when all are captured and no enemy Personnel remain, the vehicle is captured (`vehicle-captured`). A18.12: a MMC's Original 2 in vehicle CC draws a Leader Creation dr (`leaderCreation`, taken from the record, absent meaning no roll), the created leader's part as R70.46 words it. D5.42: a captor may enter the captured vehicle as its crew in the CCPh of its capture. |
| R70.35 | Which reinforcements may enter concealed? (added at 3e) | Pass 70; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-25 ("Start Pass 70 ... Run it without my intervention, up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. Any non-concealed Good Order reinforcement stack may take one non-OB "?" before it first enters (A12.12, p. 77: "Thereafter, a "?" may be placed on top of any non-concealed Good Order unit/stack before it enters the mapboard for the first time"), one per stack, never on top of another (A12.12's EXC); OB-designated "?" and Dummies stay as a card gives them. Added at 3e, widening R70.22's clause, which the agent had built to the card's OB "?" and flagged. |
| R70.36 | What does a hidden vehicle cost of an SSR's HIP allowance? (added at 3e) | Pass 70; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-25 ("Start Pass 70 ... Run it without my intervention, up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. One squad-equivalent. A12.3 (p. 80) gives the allowance in "squad-capacity" and prices no vehicle; a card whose SSR names the units it hides is read as it names them. Added at 3e. |
| R70.37 | Does lifting the TPBF refusal in a Bypassed hex make Mandatory Fire owed? (added at 3f) | Pass 70; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-25 ("Start Pass 70 ... Run it without my intervention, up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. No: A12.151 says the units in the obstacle "may" use TPBF, and forcing it would reveal concealed units. `ScenarioA1MandatoryFire.Owed` owes nothing against a stack in Bypass, as before the pass. Added at 3f. |
| R70.38 | Is a stack forced back by a Minimum Move pinned and CX? (added at 3f) | Pass 70; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-25 ("Start Pass 70 ... Run it without my intervention, up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. No: A4.134 (p. 48) pins and CXes units that "entered a hex by Minimum Move", and a unit forced back entered none. Added at 3f. |
| R70.39 | Are the Search dr and its drm public? (added at 3g) | Pass 70; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-25 ("Start Pass 70 ... Run it without my intervention, up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. Yes, with the hexes searched, though the drm shows a concealed searching stack's HS-equivalents and whether a leader leads it: A12.152 makes the dr in the open with its listed drm, and a leadership modifier applied to the Search costs the searchers' "?" (A12.141, R70.17). Accessible hexes stay R70.24's reading: the text layer has no Index definition of "Accessible". Added at 3g. |
| R70.40 | May a side inspect an enemy stack in a Location where its only units are held in Melee or broken? (added at 3h) | Pass 70; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-25 ("Start Pass 70 ... Run it without my intervention, up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. Yes: a side's units in the same Location as an enemy stack inspect it whatever their state; concealed and hidden enemy units stay as they are. A.7 leaves units held in Melee out of Good Order, so a literal A12.16 would hide the stack a side is fighting, but CC cannot be played without knowing it (A11.12, A11.14 and its example, A11.19, A11.15); A12.16 is written for viewers at a distance. Added at 3h after a read-only agent read A11 in full; since fix Round A, Dummies and prisoners inspect nothing. |
| R70.41 | May the Ambush record count each side's vehicles and BU or Stunned AFVs in the open? (added at the reviews) | Pass 70; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-25 ("Start Pass 70 ... Run it without my intervention, up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. Yes: the Ambush dr is made with its listed drm (A11.4, p. 73), and A11.19 (p. 73) has a concealed unit in CC reveal its strength before declarations. The counterpart of R70.39. Added at the reviews. |
| R70.42 | Which hexes does a Search search? (added at the reviews) | Pass 70; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-25 ("Start Pass 70 ... Run it without my intervention, up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. The hexes the player names, in his order, then the other Accessible hexes in map order, up to the Accessible count less the Final dr. A12.152 (p. 79) lets the ATTACKER choose which hexes the dr leaves unsearched; searching more costs nothing (one Casualty dr per Search dr). R70.24's "the first of the named" left the unnamed ones unsearched (the table player's finding). Added at the reviews, built at fix Round B. |
| R70.43 | Which enemy units allow a Voluntary Break? (added at the reviews) | Pass 70; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-25 ("Start Pass 70 ... Run it without my intervention, up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. The ADJACENT clause counts any Known unbroken enemy ground unit, armed or not (A10.41, p. 66: "ADJACENT to any unbroken enemy ground unit"; "armed" belongs to the LOS clause). A concealed or hidden enemy unit does not allow the break: a deviation, so a refusal tells nothing of a "?" (the table would verify by A12.16); the refusal cites this ruling, and the backlog keeps the rest. Added at the reviews; at fix Round B the LOS clause stopped counting Unarmed Personnel as armed. |
| R70.44 | Does a leader lost in a rout cause a LLMC of bystanders? (added at the reviews) | Pass 70; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-25 ("Start Pass 70 ... Run it without my intervention, up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. Yes: of the units of lower Morale Level in the Location where he is lost, bystanders included. A10.2's (p. 65) exception names Defensive First Fire only, and a rout is not DFF. R70.6's other way (a bystander leader adds no DRM to a routing unit's NMC) stands, since only A10.711's leader routs with the unit. Added at the reviews. |
| R70.45 | Is the DEFENDER offered the choice not to make a Search's or a Mopping Up's Casualty dr? (added at the reviews) | Pass 70; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-25 ("Start Pass 70 ... Run it without my intervention, up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. No: the Search Casualty dr (A12.154, p. 79) and Mopping Up's are always made when due, as R24.2 decided for Mopping Up. A deviation, with a backlog row. Added at the reviews. |
| R70.46 | What does a leader created by a MMC's Original 2 add in vehicle CC? (added at the reviews; its three cases worded at fix Round C) | Pass 70; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-25 ("Start Pass 70 ... Run it without my intervention, up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. He adds his leadership in place of a combining leader's; when a SMC that is no leader (a hero) combines, he adds none; with no SMC he adds his leadership and the +1 CCV (R29.12's reading of A18.12, p. 85, with A11.5's "No more than two units"). The CC referee's finding; added at the reviews, its three cases worded at fix Round C. |
| R70.47 | What does an Unarmed unit that fails a MC suffer? (added at the reviews) | Pass 70; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-25 ("Start Pass 70 ... Run it without my intervention, up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. Casualty Reduction instead of breaking (A20.54, p. 88: "A prisoner/unarmed unit which fails a MC is not broken; it suffers Casualty Reduction instead"; A10.3's EXC), where the attack or check carries R70.7's `unarmed` fact. Added at the reviews, built at fix Round B. |
| R70.48 | Who is told the drm of a Search's or a Mopping Up's Casualty dr? (added at fix Round E) | Pass 70; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-25 ("Start Pass 70 ... Run it without my intervention, up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. The drm is recorded with the dr (`casualtyDrm`); the record lines give it to the DEFENDER's and the adjudicator's views only, the ATTACKER's line the dr and its effect. For a Search the DEFENDER's units in a Searched hex are revealed anyway (A12.152, p. 79); for Mopping Up the drm may tell a Stealthy unit or a leader beneath a "?". The fact stays on a public record, since an owner-only record would need a new event type; a backlog row holds the rest. Added at fix Round E. |
| R71.1 | Is a pass about four times the plan's size kept whole, and in what order is it built? | Pass 71; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-26 ("Run Pass 71 without my intervention up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. Yes: it stays one pass, as R70.1 kept pass 70. The calibrated forecast was 5:41 of building (about 8:10 to the gate) against the plan's 1:20 (2:35 in all). Built in the design's increments 4a to 4g, so that whatever a session's end left out would be the last increment's (carrying a wounded man), to pass 72 with its row. Nothing was left out: the build took 5:29 by the clock, and nothing went to pass 72. |
| R71.2 | What leaves the pass? | Pass 71; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-26 ("Run Pass 71 without my intervention up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. To pass 115: Entrenching as a Sniper trigger, and the Fortification TEM and the reveal of hidden Fortifications in A14.21's comparison; to pass 90 (task 90.6): the captured MG's automatic Sniper dr at night (E1.76); to pass 135: Interior Building Locations as non-targets (A14.22); to pass 145 and Chapter G: subterranean Locations; to passes 165, 175, and 170: a wounded man's 4 MF on cavalry, motorcycles and wagons, and boats (A17.2); to the backlog: a Sniper Check by a subset, the RtPh wound pin, an empty unarmored vehicle as eligible, a 1MT AFV's Stun as Recall, an Armor Leader's Recall LLMC, G.M.D. leaders, and the early-DR order of one step. Each has a row in the backlog's section 74.1. |
| R71.3 | Which units inside a vehicle, and which vehicles, may a Sniper attack? | Pass 71; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-26 ("Run Pass 71 without my intervention up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. Only the Vulnerable: a PRC whose vehicle is CE (for a Carrier, its Passengers exposed), Partially Armored, or unarmored, and any Rider; the Inherent crew when the vehicle is CE, Partially Armored, or unarmored. An AFV with nothing Vulnerable is no target. An unarmored vehicle is itself a target, never attacked by the same dr as its PRC (A14.22, A14.33, p. 82). One Rules member decides it on facts Play reads from the index; the Partially Armored trait became a definition property. Only new plans change. |
| R71.4 | Is an unarmored vehicle with no PRC a target? | Pass 71; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-26 ("Run Pass 71 without my intervention up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. No: it is ineligible. A14.22 lets the Sniper player so treat it; a deviation where he would rather attack it, with a backlog row: an empty truck rarely beats the Alternate Target search, and offering the option at every such hex would pause play for little. Built at 4a with the Inherent crew read among the PRC, so a manned unarmored vehicle is a target. |
| R71.5 | What does a Sniper do to a crew and to a vehicle? | Pass 71; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-26 ("Run Pass 71 without my intervention up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. A dr 1 Recalls the CE crew of a fully armored AFV (D5.341, p. 204), breaks the Inherent crew of an unarmored vehicle or Partially Armored AFV, and immobilizes an unarmored vehicle (no effect if already immobilized); a dr 2 Stuns the CE crew of a fully armored AFV (D5.34, p. 203; Recall under Stun +1, D5.342) and pins such an Inherent crew. Written as the vehicle's `conditions-changed`, so the projector's Stun, Recall, and BU handling apply; the broken Inherent crew is placed broken in the vehicle and routs beneath as pass 65's `UnprotectedCrewEvents` does. |
| R71.6 | Is the enemy Sniper counter a target? | Pass 71; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-26 ("Run Pass 71 without my intervention up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. Yes (A14.31): it counts in the Alternate Target search, is part of every Location of its hex, and is a Random Selection candidate; a dr 1 eliminates it (with R71.9's SAN reduction), a dr 2 pins it. The projector clears a Sniper's pin at the CCPh's end; a pinned Sniper makes no attack and no repositioning until then. |
| R71.7 | Does SMOKE count in A14.21's TEM comparison? | Pass 71; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-26 ("Run Pass 71 without my intervention up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. Yes: the in-hex SMOKE DRM (grenades and burning wrecks) is added to each eligible target's TEM before the lowest is taken; per Location since fix Round B. The HA and Factory TEM are not in the table, so their NA already holds; Fortifications go to pass 115. |
| R71.8 | When does a Sniper attack cost a "?"? | Pass 71; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-26 ("Run Pass 71 without my intervention up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. An effective attack (dr 1 or 2) on a concealed unit costs its "?" in the LOS of a Good Order enemy ground unit, whatever the result; a break, a wound, a Reduction, or an elimination costs it always (A12.14, p. 77). A record of an attack that leaves a unit concealed names no unit. The seers narrowed to unconcealed ones by R71.21. |
| R71.9 | How does a SAN change, and when does the Sniper counter leave? | Pass 71; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-26 ("Run Pass 71 without my intervention up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. A new event `san-reduced` (side, cause) and a count of reductions on the side's state; the printed SAN kept as recorded. Rules decides the actual SAN: by day the printed less the reductions; at night the printed plus two, at most 7, less the reductions, never below the printed (E1.76, p. 225). A Sniper eliminated by a Sniper Check or by an enemy Sniper's dr 1 writes it. Read with A14.4 (p. 83) at 4b: the counter is removed when the SAN falls below 2 ("If the SAN is reduced to 1, remove the Sniper Target Selection counter"), in the same commit, and no more attacks follow; 4a had removed it always. |
| R71.10 | How is a Sniper Check offered and made? | Pass 71; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-26 ("Run Pass 71 without my intervention up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. After an effective attack the attacked side gets a pending choice, kind `sniper-check`, to take it or not; taking it commits every eligible unit (unpinned, not TI, armed, Good Order Infantry that has neither fired nor moved this Player Turn), so one combined DR has the most benefit (A14.4, p. 83). The DRM: one leader's leadership when he directs another unit, none alone (the EX); -1 each hero; -1 each crew or HS equivalent. A Final DR of 2 or less eliminates the Sniper (R71.9), 3 pins it; every participant becomes TI; the check costs a "?" by A12.141; its DR calls no Sniper. "Has fired or moved" is read from the fire markers, the MF spent, and the derived `FiredOrMovedThisPlayerTurn` (4b; composed in Rules since fix Round C). A subset goes to the backlog. When it is offered was widened by R71.18. |
| R71.11 | How is the Sniper counter given at setup? | Pass 71; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-26 ("Run Pass 71 without my intervention up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. A card game whose side has a SAN of 2 or more starts play only once that side's counter is placed, by a `sniper` argument of `asl.game.setup` after both sides have set up, the DEFENDER first. A placement is legal by A14.2: an unoccupied hex within six hexes of the most enemy-occupied hexes there can be, at most six, counted from the placing side's view (enemy "?" counted, hidden units and the enemy Sniper not). A game without a card places by hand under the same check. A game whose setup closed before the pass is never given a counter; its replay stays as it was, and the page says once that the side has no Sniper counter (A14.01). |
| R71.12 | Who makes the Sniper's choices? | Pass 71; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-26 ("Run Pass 71 without my intervention up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. The Sniper player, by a pending choice of kind `sniper` for his side, made only where more than one option exists: to attack or to forfeit and reposition (then the legal hexes); the equidistant hexes left after the lowest TEM; the Location in a hex of several; before Random Selection, the enemy Sniper, a Vulnerable Inherent Crew, or an unarmored vehicle present, or Random Selection; and which of several tied targets takes the first dr (A14.2, A14.21). The attack resumes from the rolls already recorded, and the triggers still to resolve follow in DR order. The choice is public, worded from the Sniper side's view (its reposition step the Sniper side's alone since R71.19). While it waits nothing else may happen, which is A12.14's exception (p. 78). R15.5's game picks remain only in an older record's replay. |
| R71.13 | Which DRs call a Sniper? | Pass 71; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-26 ("Run Pass 71 without my intervention up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. Every Original DR A14.1 names, found by one reader in Play over a planned step's records: fire (with `collateralCheck` and `taskCheck`, made by the target vehicle's side), ordnance (To Hit, the HE hit's IFT and checks, crew checks, Canister), `morale-check-resolved`, the Bail Out, PAATC, and both OVR NTCs; Rules names each roll's maker (`RollMaker`). It runs once per planned step through a central wrapper, in the four phases, in DR order, after the step's own effects. This supersedes R55.30's "ordnance calls no Sniper", which recorded the code rather than the rule; R15.5 had named the To Hit DR a trigger. A Sniper's own LLMC DRs are triggers too and chain (their order set by R71.25). A DR that can yield no result but a SAN is not newly guarded. Entrenching goes to pass 115. |
| R71.14 | When is a wounded SMC pinned? | Pass 71; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-26 ("Run Pass 71 without my intervention up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. A SMC wounded in the MPh after spending more than 3 MF (3 1/2 counts) is pinned (A17.2, p. 85), decided in Rules: in Fire through a new optional fact on the target (`woundPins`, absent as before), and by the planner with the wound in a Sniper attack or a Search (Mopping Up is a PFPh action, so it is never reached). The routing SMC's pin in the RtPh goes to the backlog. A broken SMC excepted since R71.27. |
| R71.15 | What does a Stun do to a SMC Temporary crew? | Pass 71; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-26 ("Run Pass 71 without my intervention up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. He is wounded with a Wound Severity dr (+1 if already wounded; a 5 or 6 eliminates) (D5.343, A17.11), rolled by the planner before the vehicle's change (`{attempt}-wound-{crew}`) and read by the projector; a record with no such dr keeps the older result. A Recall eliminates him as before. |
| R71.16 | How is a wounded man carried? | Pass 71; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-26 ("Run Pass 71 without my intervention up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. A Good Order wounded man possessing no SW may be carried by a Good Order MMC of his stack at 5 PP (A17.2, p. 85), as a `carried` argument of the move recorded on its step (absent, as before); he moves with his carrier and spends no MF of his own. Its readings stated by R71.28. |
| R71.17 | How is A18.2's "-1: CC vs AFV or per odds column < 1-1" applied? | Pass 71; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-26 ("Run Pass 71 without my intervention up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. As two causes: the -1 against an AFV is added to Leader Creation in vehicle CC when the vehicle is armored, through a new optional fact (`afvDrm`, absent as before); the per-column drm in Infantry CC was already built. G.M.D. (Chinese) leaders stay refused under R27.1 until a card brings Chinese counters. |
| R71.18 | When is a Sniper Check offered? (added at the reviews) | Pass 71; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-26 ("Run Pass 71 without my intervention up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. Whenever the attacked Location still holds units of the attacked side under "?", whether or not any may take it, and whenever any may; a "take" with no eligible unit commits nothing and says so only in the owner's view. The offer is public and tells nothing of the stack (A14.4, A14.23, A12.141). Decided at the reviews, built at fix Round A. |
| R71.19 | Who sees the forfeit's reposition hexes? (added at the reviews) | Pass 71; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-26 ("Run Pass 71 without my intervention up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. The Sniper side alone, since its own hidden units shape the set (A12.3); the other side reads only that the Sniper forfeits and repositions, then the counter's new hex, which is public (A14.2). Decided at the reviews, built at fix Round A. |
| R71.20 | Are a concealed vehicle's PRC and crew separate Sniper targets? (added at the reviews) | Pass 71; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-26 ("Run Pass 71 without my intervention up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. No: they are part of its concealed stack (A12.2: "... so does its manning Infantry or PRC, and vice versa"), one "?" candidate for A14.21 and A14.23, counted whatever the vehicle's state, the number of targets declared only once it is chosen; a "?" lost by one is lost by all. Rules decides the membership. Decided at the reviews, built at fix Round A. |
| R71.21 | Does a concealed enemy seer cost a "?" in a Sniper attack? (added at the reviews) | Pass 71; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-26 ("Run Pass 71 without my intervention up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. No: a Sniper attack, and a Sniper Check, cost a "?" only when an unconcealed Good Order enemy ground unit sees it; where every enemy seer is itself concealed, the "?" stays. A12.14 (p. 78) lets such a viewer force the loss by forfeiting its own "?" momentarily, and its sentence reaches a Sniper attack; that option is not built inside a Sniper attack, a deviation in the target's favor, with a backlog row (R70.21 built the option for movement). Decided at the reviews, built at fix Round A. |
| R71.22 | What does the other side read when a target keeps its "?"? (added at the reviews) | Pass 71; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-26 ("Run Pass 71 without my intervention up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. One neutral public result, `concealed-kept`, and an owner-only twin with the real one; the attack's effect events on that target (its conditions, a crew's Severity dr) are the owner's, and the Sniper Check's dice take its record's visibility (A12.14, A14.23). Decided at the reviews, built at fix Round A. |
| R71.23 | May a Sniper attack's Random Selection name units the Sniper side may not inspect? (added at the reviews) | Pass 71; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-26 ("Run Pass 71 without my intervention up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. Yes: it inspects its Location, so the Sniper side learns the unconcealed targets it chooses among, as A14.2's choices need them known, whatever A12.16 would withhold at a distance; a concealed stack stays one "?" (A14.23). Decided at the reviews. |
| R71.24 | May the carried man and the trigger's roll id sit on public records? (added at the reviews) | Pass 71; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-26 ("Run Pass 71 without my intervention up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. Yes: the carried man is named in the public movement step as the movers already are (the boundary of the step's record, older than the pass), and the trigger's roll id on a public Sniper record is inherent to A14.1 (the attack is public, and revisions already count withheld events). Decided at the reviews. |
| R71.25 | In what order do a Sniper's own LLMC attacks resolve? (added at the reviews) | Pass 71; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-26 ("Run Pass 71 without my intervention up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. Before that attack's Sniper Check and before any later trigger of the step (A14.3, p. 82: an attack "is considered resolved only after the full resolution of all further Sniper attacks generated by ... all LLMC/LLTC"; A14.4: the check comes "After resolving (14.3)"). R71.13's "each resolved after the attacks before it" is read so. Decided at the reviews, built at fix Round B. |
| R71.26 | How does a Sniper attack a SMC Temporary crew of a vehicle not fully armored? (added at the reviews) | Pass 71; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-26 ("Run Pass 71 without my intervention up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. As a SMC: a dr 1 eliminates him, a dr 2 wounds him (A14.3: "Eliminates SMC ... breaks Inherent crew"; a Temporary crew, A21.22, is no Inherent crew); his elimination Abandons the vehicle with his LLMC. D5.343 keeps the fully armored case. Decided at the reviews, built at fix Round B. |
| R71.27 | Is a broken SMC pinned by a wound? (added at the reviews) | Pass 71; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-26 ("Run Pass 71 without my intervention up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. No (A7.8, p. 58: "Pinning affects broken units only during Interdiction"), so R71.14's pin needs him unbroken; the pin had barred his rout. Decided at the reviews, built at fix Round B. |
| R71.28 | What does R71.16's carry read beyond the page? (added at the reviews) | Pass 71; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-26 ("Run Pass 71 without my intervention up to the merge gate"), open to a veto. A carried wounded leader still gives his carrier the two MF bonus for accompaniment (A17.2: "A wounded leader's two MF bonus for accompaniment throughout the MPh still applies"); a stack carrying a wounded man may not Double Time (stricter than the page, which bars the man only); the set-down that ends a carried man's move when his carrier is not among a later step's movers is this ruling's, not A17.2's. Decided at the reviews. |

The charge stays on a nearest unit whose route counts, in hexes stepped, no more than the next nearest group's range; a tie stays with the nearest. (added at the reviews) | Pass 72; decided under the user's word of 2026-10-27 ("Run Pass 72 without my intervention up to the merge gate."), open to a veto. A rule bar is A15.45's terrain only: a cliff or an unbridged Water Obstacle (a Blaze is not built); a building connection is ordinary movement the shortest route already prices, never a bar. The comparison applies to a nearest target only when a bar lies across its would-be route (the route that ignores bars is cheaper than the real one), against the range of the next group that has a reachable target; with none, the charge stays on the nearest. "Still closer" is strict: at a tie the targets are equidistant and the ATTACKER chooses among both groups (A15.43). A15.44's verdict needs a Known enemy unit in LOS, none reachable, and only bars keeping them out of reach; a target cut off only by other enemy Locations, or a gap of the model, keeps R30.5; a unit with no Known enemy in LOS returns to normal (A15.431) and is never hardened. ## Appendix A. The Docker Linux check

The script is committed at `src/ASL/tools/docker-check/docker_check.sh` (2026-09-29; earlier passes kept a copy in each session's scratchpad). Run it from Git Bash with Docker Desktop running, after the commit: `bash src/ASL/tools/docker-check/docker_check.sh <branch>`. It clones the committed branch into a clean `mcr.microsoft.com/dotnet/sdk:10.0` container and runs what the GitHub CI runs: a locked restore, a Release build with warnings as errors, every test project of the solution (the rules tests among them, since 2026-10-02; before then they ran as a separate step), and the source verification and pending comparison regenerations, each compared byte for byte with the committed file. Every step must report `== exit 0`. The chart supplement regeneration needs `jq`, so the merge gate runs it locally: build the Authoring CLI in Release and compare the JSON with sorted keys.

## Support Weapons review amendment RSW.1

Approved by the user on 2026-10-31: "Approve withdrawal-only CCPh drops". This amends R13.5 for new commands. A4.43 (PDF p. 50) and A11.21 (p. 73): equipment choices are part of a valid withdrawal, and commit with it. Cancelling a draft drops nothing. Historical standalone drop records retain their recorded reading. The APh window remains before that unit advances. R31d.1's best-load result is unchanged; the approved Rout workflow commits its required drops with the rout rather than offering a separate draft action.

The SW-01 combined Rules/caller delivery and its narrow claim-lock sequence were separately approved. Wiring gate and green CI are mandatory. Merge remains paused until a fresh user instruction.
